### 2026-08-03 — qa — PAS-41 CI workflow QA pass (`passenger-code 2e8dc53`): **PASS**

- **Did:** bug-ticket-style QA on the GitHub Actions build gate `ios-developer` added and `ios-code-reviewer` already APPROVEd (both entries immediately below). Read `BOARD.md` in full and this file's recent worklog (both entries) first, per protocol. No PRD/TRD/TEST-PLAN for this task — it's infra, matches the reviewer's own N/A calls. Two things independently verified rather than trusted from either prior entry: the build reproduces, and the YAML is well-formed.
- **Build — independently reproduced, not reused from `ios-developer`'s or the reviewer's own local runs.** Ran the exact CI command on a dedicated, previously-unused `-derivedDataPath` in scratch (not a shared/default one): `xcodebuild build -project Passenger.xcodeproj -scheme Passenger -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED**, `grep -c "error:"` on the full log → `0`. Checked `ps aux`/`xcrun simctl list devices` first per L-029 — three other sessions had concurrent `xcodebuild` processes and booted simulators (`T032-Accept`, `T051-Verify-*`) at the time, but `generic/platform=iOS Simulator` is a device-less destination (no simulator boot required), so this run had no UDID-contention exposure regardless; disk was healthy for this run (`df -h /`: 37Gi avail, 24% — the ENOSPC condition other sessions hit earlier today had cleared).
- **YAML well-formedness — checked with a generic parser, not GitHub's own.** `ruby -ryaml` (pyyaml wasn't installed, same substitute `ios-developer` used) parses the file cleanly into the expected structure: `name`, `on: {push: {branches: [main]}, pull_request: {branches: [main]}}`, one job (`build`, `runs-on: macos-15`) with three steps (Checkout, Select latest stable Xcode, Build). One thing worth naming, not a defect: a generic YAML 1.1 loader resolves the bare `on:` key to the boolean `true` rather than the string `"on"` (the well-known YAML "Norway problem") — cosmetic in the parsed dict, and not a GitHub Actions bug, since GitHub's own workflow schema special-cases `on:` as the trigger key and every workflow file in existence uses it unquoted this way. No `actionlint` available in this environment to cross-check against GitHub's actual schema; the generic-parser pass plus direct visual inspection of triggers/runner/steps against the ticket's stated spec is what this verdict rests on.
- **Independently re-confirmed the "nothing pushed yet" claim** rather than taking the two build entries' word for it: `git fetch origin main` + `git log origin/main..HEAD` shows `2e8dc53` and its parent `d537ca5` both ahead of `origin/main`; `git branch -r --contains 2e8dc53` returns nothing. Confirms the DoD item "a real push/PR triggers it and completes successfully" is still open — expected, not a QA failure, per the dispatch brief.
- **Verdict: PASS.** No blocking findings, nothing new to add to the reviewer's non-blocking minors list (timeout-minutes, concurrency group, DerivedData caching — not re-litigated here, still open for whoever next touches this file). This is the last internal gate before `chief-of-staff` reports to Aviran; the live-Actions-run DoD item stays open until he pushes.

### 2026-08-03 — ios-code-reviewer — PAS-41 CI workflow review (`passenger-code 2e8dc53`): **APPROVE**

- **Did:** first review of `.github/workflows/ios-build.yml` (new file, 33 lines), the build-only GitHub Actions gate `ios-developer` added at `passenger-code 2e8dc53`. Read `BOARD.md` in full and this file's recent worklog first, per protocol — found no `BOARD.md` row for PAS-41 (this task isn't tracked there, only in the worklog and Linear), so nothing to move on that board; confirmed via `grep -n "PAS-41\|CI\|ios-build" agent-os/BOARD.md` (zero hits). No PRD/TRD exists for this task (it's infra, not a feature) — TRD-conformance pass N/A. No UI/HIG/Liquid Glass surface touched — those passes N/A. `/vibe-security` pass: clean, no secrets/keys/tokens in the file, no auth/payment/location code touched.
- **Verified against the live project rather than trusting the commit message:** `xcodebuild -list -project Passenger.xcodeproj` confirms `Passenger` is the project's one and only scheme, matching the workflow's `-scheme Passenger`. `grep -c XCRemoteSwiftPackageReference Passenger.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj` → `0` — no remote SPM packages, so the build needs no network dependency resolution beyond `actions/checkout@v4`, removing a whole class of CI flakiness. `SupabaseConfig.plist` is gitignored and referenced only at runtime in `AppConfig.swift`/`BuildPhase.swift` (`grep -rn SupabaseConfig Passenger/`) — its absence in a fresh CI checkout cannot break `xcodebuild build`, since the workflow only compiles, never runs the app.
- **`xcode-version: latest-stable` vs. pinning (the ios-developer's flagged [ASSUMPTION]) — checked, and it's the right call, not just a defensible one.** `project.pbxproj` shows `IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET = 26.0`, `SWIFT_VERSION = 6.0`, `SWIFT_STRICT_CONCURRENCY = complete` on both build configs — this project targets iOS 26 exclusively, no backward-compat path (matches the fleet's standing "no `if #available`" rule elsewhere). Pinning to an arbitrary specific Xcode number risks landing on one that predates the iOS 26 SDK and hard-failing every run for a reason unrelated to the code being tested; `latest-stable` is the only choice that tracks the SDK the project actually requires. The one residual, unverifiable-from-here risk: whether the `macos-15` runner's current `latest-stable` Xcode already ships the iOS 26 SDK — that's the same "first real push is the actual test" gap `ios-developer` already disclosed in `ONBOARDING.md`, not a defect in this YAML.
- **Build command and destination:** `xcodebuild build -project Passenger.xcodeproj -scheme Passenger -destination 'generic/platform=iOS Simulator' CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` is correct for a build-only gate. `generic/platform=iOS Simulator` avoids depending on any specific simulator runtime/device being preinstalled on the runner image — the right choice given the job never launches or tests the app. `CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO` is required and correctly scoped, since there's no Apple Developer account yet (`ACCOUNTS-AND-COSTS.md`). Trigger scope (`push`/`pull_request` to `main` only) matches the ticket's stated goal of a green build gate, nothing more.
- **Non-blocking minors:** no `timeout-minutes` on the job (GitHub's default is a 6-hour ceiling on hosted runners — cheap insurance against a hung build silently burning Actions minutes); no `concurrency` group to auto-cancel a superseded run on rapid successive pushes; no DerivedData/build caching (low-value here specifically, since there's no SPM network fetch to cache around, but would still speed up repeat builds later). None of these block a first, minimal, "build gate only" gate — worth picking up if/when this workflow grows (e.g. once tests get folded in).
- **Noted, not re-litigated:** the `architect` entry immediately above this one (T-053/`PAS-41` search-quick-filters TRD) already flags that its own Linear ID and this task's Linear ID collide — one of the two is mislabelled. Already surfaced there as `chief-of-staff`'s to resolve in Linear; not re-flagged as a new finding here, just cross-referenced so it isn't lost between the two entries.
- **Verdict: APPROVE.** No blocking findings, no minors that need a fix before this ships. Small, correct, low-risk mechanical change — reviewed to that bar, not padded with invented risk. Moving to `qa`, briefed that there is nothing to behaviorally test here beyond watching the first real Actions run go green once Aviran pushes — the actual open item is the same one `ios-developer` already named: no live GitHub Actions run has been observed yet from any session.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry). Explicit path. No `passenger-code` changes (review only). Not pushed — Aviran-gated, `CLAUDE.md` rule 9. Hash in the final response.

### 2026-08-03 — architect — T-053/`PAS-43` search-quick-filters TRD §9: six vacuous pass conditions fixed, two standing rules added (TRD v2)

**Correction, `chief-of-staff`, same day:** this entry originally read `T-053/PAS-41`. `PAS-41` is a real, unrelated, already-in-progress `ios-developer` task ("CI workflow file doesn't exist...") — T-053 had no Linear issue at all before this correction. Verified directly against Linear (`list_issues`, `get_issue PAS-41`), not inferred. Filed **`PAS-43`** for T-053 retroactively, status Done, commit cited in a comment. See item 4 below (architect's own flag) and the CI-workflow entry a few entries down, which independently cross-referenced the same collision.

- **Did:** `prds/search-quick-filters/TRD.md` → **v2** (header changelog table, **D14**, §4.10 and §9 amended). **The two filed rows:** §9 **row 4(a)**'s "every other `PlaceLayer`/`HoodLayer` receives `isDimmed == true`" was vacuously true over an empty set — at `.cityWide`/`.neighborhood` no `PlaceLayer` is constructed at all, so it held in exactly the case PRD req 4 failed. Rewritten to assert the **resolved fill alpha** (`HoodLayer.fillColor` → `UIColor.cgColor.alpha`) of all 24 `HoodLayer`s at **both** `.cityWide` (span 0.14, the cold-open camera) and `.close`, with element counts stated before any ∀, the matched Hood's band required non-`nil` (else BLOCKED, not passed), and the `PlaceLayer` count asserted **`0`** at `.cityWide` — the tier the v1 condition was vacuous at is now the tier the check is required to cover. §9 **row 8(c)** split into **8(c-i)** result rows and **8(c-ii)** chips, the chip case naming `.fixedSize` explicitly and stating that a `.lineLimit` grep cannot see `HStack` overflow. **The sweep the board asked for found four more of the same class, all fixed in this pass rather than left for a third occurrence:** **4(b)** (`emphasis == nil` asserted the input to the dim, never that the dim lifted → rendered readback of the 23 previously-dimmed alphas); **3(c)** ("result ⊆ `.all`" **and** "every place is `.eatDrink`" both hold over `[]` → pinned to the exact expected array, derived from the shipped seed: `run("b", .only(.eatDrink))` = `[Bavli(Hood), Anna Loulou Bar, HaMakolet, Suzanne Restaurant]` vs `.all`'s five, so the chip drops exactly `Carmel Market Spice Corner` and keeps the Hood — D8 made falsifiable); **8(a)** ("**every one** reports ≥44pt" is vacuous over zero elements → exact counts first); **2(d)** ("≤16ms per call" is trivially met by a matcher returning `[]` → the timed call must return a stated non-zero count). §9 preamble gained **two standing rules**: no pass condition may be satisfiable over an empty set, and every negative-existence check needs a positive control — **11 sub-checks in this table are `grep … → 0 hits`**, which passes identically whether the symbol is absent or the grep covered nothing. **§4.10 also brought in line with the system as built** (`architect.md`: the doc in git must describe the system as built): the fill's dim as a numeric composition via `HeatPalette.fillOpacity(for:dimmedBy:)`, and a place match emphasising `place.hoodID` because `PlaceLayer` is zoom-gated on `showsNames` and `HoodLayer` is not.

- **Did:** a fresh, full code-review of `passenger-code d537ca5` against `prds/search-quick-filters/TRD.md` and its intersection with `prds/time-slider/TRD.md`/`prds/live-events-overlay/TRD.md` (`HoodLayer`, `MapScreen`) — required because the prior verdict at this gate was a blocking REJECT (T-035/T-036 convention). Not diff-scoped: read `HoodLayer.swift`, `HeatPalette.swift`, `SearchDim.swift`, `CategoryChipRow.swift`, `MapScreen.swift`, and `Place.swift`/`Hood.swift` in their current state, not just the commit's hunks.
- **F1a (heat-fill dim) — independently traced, genuinely fixed.** `HoodLayer.fillColor` (now `internal`, not `private`, so `HoodLayerFillDimTests` can read it directly) is `HeatPalette.hue.opacity(HeatPalette.fillOpacity(for: band, dimmedBy: dimOpacity))`, and `fillOpacity(for:dimmedBy:)` is `opacity(for: band) * dimOpacity` — a pure numeric composition, not a doc-comment claim. `dimOpacity` is `isDimmed ? 0.25 : 1`, the same value the stroke/annotation already used. Traced the full call path, not the commit message. `HeatPaletteTests`/`HoodLayerFillDimTests` (both new) assert the *resolved* `UIColor` alpha is weaker when dimmed, across every band and zoom tier, plus a same-fill-when-undimmed regression guard and a stays-`.clear`-either-way case for no band — this is exactly the "rendered result, not the passed value" standard L-009 asked for, not a re-run of the same shallow assertion that missed it the first time.
- **F1b (place-only match at cold-open zoom) — independently traced, genuinely fixed, and it's the alternate mechanism product's own REJECT text named as "the obvious candidate."** `MapScreen.swift`'s `HoodLayer` `ForEach` (around line 224) is not gated by `showsNames`/zoom tier at all — only the `PlaceLayer` `ForEach` a few lines below is (`if showsNames`, span < 0.06). `SearchDim.emphasis` now adds `place.hoodID` to the `hoods` set for every place match (`Passenger/Search/SearchDim.swift:37`), so `MapScreen.isHoodDimmed` returns `false` for that Hood regardless of pin visibility. Net effect at the shipped cold-open camera (`telAvivCityWide`, span 0.14, `.cityWide`): the matched place's own Hood renders its heat fill at full strength while the other 23 Hoods dim to 0.25 — a real, visible, zoom-independent change. `place.hoodID: String` / `Hood.id: String` types line up (confirmed by reading both structs); this compiles, not just typechecks in my head. `MapScreen.swift` itself is untouched by this commit, which is correct: the fix works around `PlaceLayer`'s zoom gate rather than removing it, matching product's own stated fix direction rather than a novel deviation from the TRD.
- **F2 (chip truncation) — genuinely fixed, mirrors precedent exactly.** `CategoryChip`'s `Text(category.displayName)` now carries `.fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)`, byte-identical to the modifier `SearchResultRow.swift` already carries on its two text runs (an already-shipped, already-reviewed pattern, not an invented one). **One non-blocking gap:** `SearchRowGrowthGuardTests` (pre-existing, not touched by this commit) asserts `.lineLimit(` absence across all search files and asserts `SearchResultRow`'s own `Text`-run-count-equals-`.fixedSize`-count, but nothing equivalent exists for `CategoryChipRow` — so a future edit to `CategoryChip` could silently drop `.fixedSize` again with no test catching it. - **P0 spot-check (not full re-derivation, per dispatch brief — 7 of 8 already passed twice):** the diff's surface is narrow (a fill-color computation, a `Set` insertion, one Text modifier) and touches no routing, location-state, session-persistence, or accessibility-label code; `CategoryChip`'s existing `.frame(minHeight: 44)`/`.accessibilityLabel` are unchanged by this commit. No regression risk found on read.
- **`/vibe-security` pass:** clean. No new network calls, no new persistence, no logging, no secrets — the whole diff is pure rendering math (`Double`/`Color` composition) and a `Set<Hood.ID>` insertion from data already on-device.
- **Build:** independently run (not trusted from the commit message) — `xcodebuild -project Passenger.xcodeproj -scheme Passenger -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17e' build` → **BUILD SUCCEEDED**, no errors, no warnings in the touched files.
- **Test — could not be completed, environment-caused, disclosed rather than papered over.** The build machine's root volume was at 99%+ capacity this session (`df -h /`: 266Mi free at first check, dropped to single-digit Mi and then a hard `ENOSPC` mid-session — my own Bash tool started failing on `echo hi`). A full `xcodebuild test` failed with `IDETesting: ... fileSystemFailure(reason: "FileBackedCASDatabase: rename failed: No space left on device")` and `CASTreeDataStructure/Importer.swift: Fatal error: ... mkstemp: No space left on device` — not a single real `XCTAssert...failed` line anywhere, and explicitly **not** the L-029 "Restarting after unexpected exit, crash, or test timeout" pattern; this is disk exhaustion, not simulator-UDID contention. A scoped retry (`-only-testing` on just the three new/changed test files) also failed to even complete for the same reason. Did not attempt to free space by deleting shared `DerivedData` — other sessions were concurrently building (multiple booted simulators observed, consistent with the documented L-029 contention pattern) and unilaterally clearing shared build state risked breaking a concurrent session's in-flight work, which is outside this review's scope to risk. **Confidence the tests would pass regardless:** traced by hand — `HeatPaletteTests`/`HoodLayerFillDimTests` assert `dimmedAlpha < undimmedAlpha`, which holds unconditionally since `opacity(for: band) > 0` for every band and `dimOpacity` is either `0.25` or `1`; `SearchDimTests`' new cases are plain `Set`/dictionary equality checks against code already read in full above. High confidence, not proof — flagged plainly as unexecuted rather than reported as green.

Two non-blocking minors (both above): the `CategoryChipRow` structural-guard gap, and the unexecuted full test run (environment-caused, not a code defect). Moving T-038 to `qa` — flagging for `qa` that this session never got a real green full-suite run, so their pass should be the first one, and that F1a/F1b are best verified live at the app's actual cold-open camera (search "suzan" or similar place-only query, confirm the matched place's Hood visibly holds its heat fill while surrounding Hoods dim) rather than from unit-test evidence alone.

### 2026-08-03 — product — T-038/PAS-29 acceptance pass: **REJECT** — req 4's dim emphasises nothing at the app's own cold-open zoom, and no test anywhere asserts a layer's dim

- **Did:** acceptance pass on T-038 (Search & quick filters) against `prds/search-quick-filters/search-quick-filters.md` and `TRD.md` v1, dispatched after `ios-code-reviewer` APPROVE WITH MINORS and `qa` PASS. Read `BOARD.md`'s two T-038 rows and the `qa` entry below before starting. **Re-derived all eight P0s from `passenger-code` source directly**, per this workspace's standing acceptance discipline — not a re-read of the two upstream verdicts.
  - **Commit tested:** `passenger-code 3d8e3b8`. The brief's "confirmed unchanged by `beb3f02`" was already stale on arrival — `passenger-code 2267090` (`qa`'s own new tests) and `passenger-code 198b082` (T-032's time-slider build) have since landed, and **198b082 rewrites `MapScreen.swift` (+264/−) and `MapNavRow.swift`**, both search-carrying files. Re-derived against current `main` as well as the pinned commit; the search wiring survived T-032 intact (`SearchOverlay` at z5, z3 tap-catcher at `MapScreen.swift:332`, `MapNavRow` search button, `isDimmed` on both layers all present and correct after the merge). No T-032 regression on T-038. Worth stating because a pinned-hash brief plus a fast-moving `main` is how a verdict ends up describing a tree nobody is running.
  1. **Seven of eight P0s hold**, each re-derived rather than accepted: req 1 (one door — `openSearch`/`dismissSearch`/`handlePresentedSurfaceChange` read directly, `grep` for `fullScreenCover|NavigationStack|NavigationLink` over `Search/`+`SearchSheet/` → 0); req 2 (three match kinds — `SearchQuery.run`'s five rules read line by line, visible `Text("Search")` label distinct from the `TextField` prompt); req 3 (two chips, one call site, `.fresh == .all`, checkmark-not-colour selected state); req 5 (routing — both branches call the same `router.openPlace`/`openHood` a pin or polygon tap makes; exactly two real `.sheet(isPresented:` sites app-wide, the other two `grep` hits being `DetailRouter`'s own doc comments); req 6 (four states present; `grep` for `CoreLocation|LocationStore|CLLocation` → 0, so location-denied is structurally a no-op); req 7 (session lives on `MapScreen` as `@State` and `handlePresentedSurfaceChange` takes no `session` parameter — an interruption *cannot* clear it, Poka-Yoke rather than a tested behaviour; all four manual-dismiss paths verified by call site to route through the one `dismissSearch`); req 8 partially — 44pt frames and both pinned VoiceOver strings are real and correct.
  2. **F1 — BLOCKING, PRD req 4 bullet 1. Two compounding halves; together, opening search and typing a match changes nothing a user can see.**
     - **F1a — the Hood dim never reaches the heat fill.** `HoodLayer.dimOpacity` is applied to the polygon's *stroke* (`.opacity(0.35 * dimOpacity)` / `Color("Flag").opacity(dimOpacity)`) and to `annotationContent`, but `MapPolygon.foregroundStyle(fillColor)` is left untouched in all three `effectiveStroke` branches, and `HeatPalette.fill(for:)` returns `hue.opacity(opacity(for: band))` — a fixed per-band alpha with no dim factor. So a non-matching Hood renders its **full-strength heat fill** while search is open. The fill is the dominant visual and the channel req 4 bullet 1 is actually about; what dims is a 0.5pt hairline and a label that is invisible at city-wide zoom anyway. The file's own doc comments assert the opposite **twice** — *"`true` multiplies every visible element's opacity by 0.25"* and *"the one multiplier every visible element in this layer applies"* — so this reads as intended behaviour that was never wired, not a deliberate scoping call.
     - **F1b — a place-only match has no pin to emphasise at the zoom the app opens at.** `MapScreen.swift:244` gates the `PlaceLayer` `ForEach` on `showsNames`, i.e. `zoomTier == .close`, i.e. latitude span **< 0.06**. The shipped cold-open camera is `Self.telAvivCityWide`, span **0.14** → `.cityWide` → **no place pin is constructed at all**. `SearchDim.emphasis` returns `(places: {ids}, hoods: [])` for such a match, so no Hood is emphasised either.
     - **Rendered result at cold open, F1a + F1b together:** heat fills unchanged, hairline strokes marginally fainter, no pins. The map is visually indistinguishable from before the search. Reproduce in ~15 seconds: cold open → search → type `"suzan"` (2 place results, 0 Hood results, both confirmed by `SearchShippedSeedTests`). **Surface:** F1a at every zoom and every query; F1b at every zoom with span ≥ 0.06 (`.cityWide` **and** `.neighborhood`) — the app's opening state for everyone and the *permanent* state for a user who denies location, which PRD req 6 bullet 5 explicitly supports. Not a corner case; it is the primary path for the most common query type.
     - **Correction, disclosed rather than quietly rewritten.** The first version of this entry (`passenger-brain 8545d2b`) described F1 as *"every Hood polygon/stroke/annotation at 0.25 opacity, zero pins, nothing bright."* That was **wrong** — it took `dimOpacity`'s doc comment at its word instead of tracing `fillColor` through `HeatPalette`. The verdict does not change and the requirement still fails, but the mechanism is close to the inverse of what was first written, and F1a is the larger half of the defect. Caught on this agent's own critic pass before delivery (`CLAUDE.md`'s writing rules), corrected in this entry's follow-up commit (hash in the Git line below). Worth recording as its own small lesson: I rejected this build for asserting a passed value instead of a rendered one, and then made the same class of mistake in the rejection by trusting a doc comment instead of the render path.
  3. **Why five gates passed it, and the fix that outlives this task (L-009).** TRD §9 row 4(a)'s pass condition is *"that place's id and that Hood's id are the only members; every other `PlaceLayer`/`HoodLayer` receives `isDimmed == true`"* — every clause of which is **true in the failing case**. It asserts the value handed to a layer, never what the layer draws. And it was never implemented anyway: `grep -rn "isDimmed" PassengerTests/ PassengerUITests/` returns **0**, and `grep` for `showsNames|zoomTier|cityWide` across all five search test files returns **0**. So the requirement had no falsifiable check at any layer, and `SearchDimTests` (green, 3 cases) tests set membership on the pure function while the composition that breaks sits in `MapScreen`. Per L-006/L-009 I wrote the missing criteria into the PRD **in this same commit**, stated as the rendered consequence: req 4 gains *"whenever results show, at least one map element renders undimmed — at every zoom the map can be at, including the cold-open zoom"*, and req 8 gains the chip case. Both labelled as added at acceptance with the reason.
  4. **F2 — non-blocking, fold into the same fix pass, PRD req 8 bullet 3.** `CategoryChipRow` is a fixed `HStack` with no wrap and no horizontal scroll, and neither chip's `Text(category.displayName)` carries `.fixedSize`. At the largest accessibility size `.subheadline` scales roughly 15pt → ~42pt; "Eat & Drink" and "Things to do" plus two checkmarks plus 28pt of horizontal padding each is well over 600pt against a ~393pt width, so both labels compress and truncate. `qa`'s new `SearchRowGrowthGuardTests` sweeps for `.lineLimit(` and correctly finds none — but `.lineLimit` is not the mechanism here; `HStack` overflow is, and only `.fixedSize` prevents it. `SearchResultRow` has `.fixedSize` on both `Text` runs; `CategoryChip` has it on neither. **Stated as a code-read finding, not a live one** — `qa` could not force a content-size override in that session and neither could I, so this is reasoning from the layout, not an observed screenshot.
  5. **Four `architect` questions were routed to `product` at TRD §12 and are all ruled**, in the PRD's Decisions log: **D8 upheld** (a chip narrows *places*; a Hood match survives — req 3 says "narrows," a Hood carries no `PlaceCategory`, and dropping Hoods would make a chip delete a whole result kind); **D9 upheld** (two row words; a keyword hit is a Place row — req 2 bullet 2's own justification clause is a two-way distinction and req 8's VoiceOver examples give only two forms); **§4.10's undimmed MapKit base tiles accepted as a limitation** (the requirement is about the prominence of Passenger-authored content; `Map` exposes no layer to insert one into and a full-screen scrim would darken the matches); **D2 (0.45/0.92) accepted for Build Phase 1 and carried to Aviran**, whose call the layout is (§9 Q15) — the architect priced the reversal at two constants and one gesture, so nothing is locked. **Not ruled:** D10's underlying interrupted-vs-completed product rule (`ux-flows.md` §9 Q17) — flagged by `designer` as its own reconciliation rather than Aviran's call, so it stays Aviran's and is not `product`'s to close alone. The build matches the PRD as written either way.
  6. **Scope gate re-run on inherited work** (Job 0 item 4 — a task already in the pipeline gets the citation check the first time `product` touches it at a gate). Both Motivation quotes verified **verbatim** against `strategy/passenger-strategy.md`: the "Search + quick filters… search filters the map, it doesn't bypass it" line at strategy line 47, and the "surfaced as **quick filters** inside the search sheet" line at line 36. Authorized V1 scope. No social surface, no onboarding, no Phase-2/3 hooks anywhere in `Search/` or `SearchSheet/`. **Data/schema need** (Job 1 item 7): already explicit and already owned — `places.keywords` is specified in `places-dataset` req 4 with the probe list as T-042 step B4, and the PRD's Technical design states the three matched fields by name. Nothing to fix on that pass.
- **Verdict: REJECT.** Back to `build`, `ios-developer`. Fix F1; fold in F2. Fix *direction* deliberately not prescribed — emphasising a matched place's own Hood is the obvious candidate (`SearchIndex.PlaceEntry` already carries both `hoodName` and `place.hoodID`, so the data is in hand), but ungating matched pins from `showsNames` or moving the camera are live alternatives and the call is `architect`/`ios-developer`'s.
- **Evidence:** all greps and line numbers above run against `passenger-code` current `main` and cross-checked at `3d8e3b8`; `git show --stat 3d8e3b8`, `git diff --stat 3d8e3b8..HEAD`. No build or test run was needed to establish F1 — it follows from two constants in shipped source (`MapZoomTier.closeSpanThreshold = 0.06`, `telAvivCityWide` span `0.14`) and one `if` at `MapScreen.swift:244`.
- **Left behind:**
  - **`architect` owes a TRD §9 amendment** — filed as **T-053** on `BOARD.md` with `architect` as owner rather than left in this entry (`CLAUDE.md` rule 5): row 4(a) and row 8(c) both assert a passed value instead of a rendered result. **Flagged as a class, not an instance** — this is L-009's second occurrence after T-046/LOC-104, so the sweep across other shipped TRDs' §9 tables matters more than the two rows.
  - **The live visual dim is still unconfirmed by a human**, carried from `qa`'s entry below — and F1 is precisely the kind of thing a 30-second look at a real device would have caught before code review. Worth noting that the environment gap and the escaped defect are the same gap.
  - `TEST-PLAN.md` row 4 will need rebuilding once the amended TRD §9 lands; I did not edit `qa`'s artifact.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `prds/search-quick-filters/search-quick-filters.md` (req 4 + req 8 bullets, 6 Decisions-log rows, Status → Draft v2), `agent-os/BOARD.md` (both T-038 rows + new T-053 row), `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry). Explicit paths, two commits: **`passenger-brain 8545d2b`** (verdict) then a follow-up correcting the F1 mechanism (see item 2's last bullet — the second commit exists because the first was wrong, and says so rather than being squashed away). **Rule-2 note:** the BOARD.md half of that correction was swept into `passenger-brain 92b4b3d`, a concurrent T-037/PAS-28 session's commit, before this agent could stage it — same shape as the `0dd3d21` incident `CLAUDE.md` rule 2 documents. Named here rather than silently absorbed; nothing of that session's was carried the other way by this agent. Committed, **not pushed** — Aviran-gated, `CLAUDE.md` rule 9. No `passenger-code` change — this is a verdict, not a fix.

### 2026-08-03 — ios-developer — T-032/PAS-15 build: Time slider, Build Phase 1 (C1-C14) — full TRD v3 scope landed

- **Did:** built the full TRD v3 scope (`prds/time-slider/TRD.md`, `trd-review` cleared 2026-08-02, both signoffs APPROVE). Read `BOARD.md`'s T-032 rows and `PROGRESS.md`'s recent worklog first, per protocol — the rows correctly flagged this build as orphaned (5+ rounds uncommitted, no known live spawner) and named two genuine partial-WIP files in the shared tree. Confirmed both directly rather than assuming: `Passenger/Map/SettingsHint.swift`'s modification is a harmless `Text`-concatenation→interpolation refactor (`git diff`, byte-for-byte matches the brief's description) — kept unmodified, folded into this commit. `Passenger/Support/HeatRepaintSignpost.swift` is a real, well-formed signpost helper already matching its own eventual TRD §4.7 contract exactly (`begin()`/`endIfPending()`, mirrors `ColdOpenSignpost.swift`) — built on as-is rather than reimplemented.
  - **C1 (`MapChromeState`/`NavSurface`)** was already correct — a concurrent T-036/T-038 session created it first in the shared tree, per the TRD's own §4.1 tripwire comment predicting exactly this. Verified byte-for-byte against TRD §4.1 before treating it as done; added nothing.
  - **C2** — `Passenger/Map/HeatButton.swift` (new, `flame.fill`, icon-only per D6, mirrors `SearchButton`/`ProfileButton`'s 44×44 `.thinMaterial` circle convention exactly) added to the already-existing `MapNavRow.swift` (which only had Search/Profile from concurrent builds) rather than recreating the row. Bucket-2 fade (`NearMeButton`/`HoodButton`/`SettingsHint`) wired in `MapScreen.swift` via a shared `.opacity(chrome.isPresenting ? 0 : 1).allowsHitTesting(!chrome.isPresenting)` on their existing container — the TRD's own §2.3 comment in `MapScreen.swift` had explicitly deferred this to "T-032's own build."
  - **C3-C5** — `Passenger/HeatModal/{HourFormat,HourSlider,HourReadout,HeatModalCard}.swift`. `HeatModalCard` owns its own scrim (`Color.black.opacity(0.3)`, drag-to-dismiss, `.move(edge:.bottom)` transition collapsing under Reduce Motion) — discovered mid-build that `PlacesListOverlay`/`PassportSurface` (both built by concurrent sessions) already cite `HeatModalCard` by name as "the pattern they mirror," so this build matched their exact construction instead of inventing a third near-duplicate scrim mechanism.
  - **C6/C7** — `Passenger/Map/HeatComposition.swift` (pure `[Hood]`×hour→`[HoodFill]`) + `MapScreen`'s new `hoodFills` computed property, replacing the old per-Hood inline `densityStore.band(...)` call in the `Map`'s `ForEach`. `HeatRepaintSignpost.begin()` fires on a real hour change from either writer (`HourSlider`'s custom `Binding` setter, `EdgeHourZone`'s drag handler); `endIfPending()` fires immediately after `hoodFills` resolves.
  - **C8** — `DensityStore.refreshIfHourRolled()` gained two new call sites (`MapScreen.handleHeatButtonTap` on modal open, `EdgeHourZone.onTouchDown` on edge touch-down) plus the mid-`await` guard (capture `selectedHour` before `await load()`, skip the remap if it changed during the await). Guard covered by a test that smuggles a concurrent-edit simulation through the injected `now()` closure's second invocation — deterministic, no real concurrency needed.
  - **C9** — four new colour sets (`PillSurface`/`SliderFill`/`NowTick`/`EdgeRail`; `MutedOnSurface` already existed) — hex values computed and verified against the real WCAG formula before authoring, then re-verified by `HeatModalContrastTests` reading the actual compiled asset catalog (not the computed values assumed correct).
  - **C10** — `Passenger/Density/DensitySeed.swift` + `Resources/density-seed-tel-aviv.json` (5 real Hoods — florentin/kerem-hateimanim/neve-tzedek/lev-hair/old-north — satisfying the authoring rule: 4-6 transitions each for 3+ Hoods, 2 null hours on old-north) + `DensityStore.load()`'s new unconditional `BuildPhase.seedIsAuthoritative` branch, mirroring `PlaceCatalog.load()` exactly per the TRD.
  - **C11-C14** — `Passenger/EdgeHour/{EdgeGeometry,EdgeAvailability,EdgeHint,EdgeHourZone,EdgeHourTrack}.swift`. `EdgeGeometry`/`EdgeAvailability` are pure with full unit-test matrices (no simulator). `EdgeHourZone`'s `DragGesture(minimumDistance: 4)` latches vertical-dominance once via `@GestureState`, in the default `.local` coordinate space (v3's own correction); it only exists in `MapScreen`'s hierarchy when `EdgeAvailability.liveEdges(...)` includes that edge — structural removal, not an `isEnabled` flag, per D7 rule (c).
- **Disclosed deviation, not silent:** C10's literal "mirror `PlaceCatalog.load()` exactly" instruction makes `BuildPhase.seedIsAuthoritative`'s unconditional short-circuit fire on every `DensityStore.load()` call, with no per-instance override — identical to `PlaceCatalog`'s own check. This makes three of T-031's original `DensityStoreTests` (`liveLoadResolvesBand`, `failedFetchNoCacheIsUnavailable`, `failedFetchWithCacheFallsBack`, all built around injecting a `FakeDensityAPI`) unreachable through `load()` regardless of what the fakes are told to do — the TRD's own §4.5 preamble asks that these "pass unmodified alongside the new cases," which cannot hold in the literal sense once C10 is built as specified. `PlaceCatalogTests.swift` already carries this exact resolution for the identical shape of problem (its own header: "the live/cache branches are built to spec... but deliberately not exercised here"). Replaced the three with `seedIsAuthoritativeAfterLoad`/`loadMakesZeroFetchAttemptsWhileSeedIsAuthoritative`, direct precedents of `PlaceCatalogTests`' own `sourceIsSeedAfterLoad`/`loadMakesZeroFetchAttempts`. No unique coverage lost — `DensitySnapshot(rows:)`'s parsing/boundary-validation logic (what those three tests actually exercised) is independently and fully covered in `DensitySnapshotTests.swift`. Documented in the test file's own header for whoever reviews next, not left for a diff to explain on its own.
- **The TRD's own blocking condition, verified as a real test, not asserted by construction:** §9 row 7(e)/C13 says the step "is not done until" an in-band vertical edge drag is shown to move `selectedHour` while leaving `camera`/`MKCoordinateRegion` byte-identical — D7's whole hit-test argument is a prediction the TRD itself says needs confirming, not trusting. No glass-on-glass stacking, no `if #available(iOS ...)` version-gating, no `@StateObject`/`@ObservedObject` anywhere in the diff (`grep` clean on all three).
- **Verdict: REQUEST CHANGES.** One blocking finding (`ProfileButton`'s missing contrast backing, item 7 above) — everything else (TRD conformance, L-028, Liquid Glass conformance, test coverage and its independent live verification, `/vibe-security`) is APPROVE-quality. ### 2026-08-03 — architect — T-036 §9 row 6 + T-034 §3.4 clause 9: two documentation-only TRD amendments, no re-review owed

- **Did:** the two TRD amendments `product` left behind at its T-034/T-035/T-036 acceptance pass (that entry's "Left behind" item 4), dispatched by `chief-of-staff` round 6. Read `BOARD.md`'s T-034/T-036/T-051 rows and this file's recent worklog (the `product` acceptance entry and the `ios-code-reviewer` T-038 entry) before touching either file. Both amendments reconcile a TRD to a PRD that already said the thing — **neither changes a requirement, schema, contract, D-decision, or build step, so neither is a re-review trigger.** Same class as the closed T-049/`PAS-37` amendment.
  1. **`prds/places-been-saved/TRD.md` §9 row 6** (T-036). Pass condition (a) now reads "…icon, line, and a CTA that dismisses the list — not an error string, not a spinner — **and its copy names what would fill the list** (how a place gets here: save one, or spend time at one), not only that the list is empty. **A string that states emptiness alone — 'Nothing here yet.' — FAILS this row**." The old text checked icon + line + CTA and had silently dropped PRD req 6 bullet 1's "naming what would fill it," which is why `qa` tested a weaker bar than the PRD sets and had nothing to fail against the shipped `PlacesListEmptyState.swift` — `product` caught it only at acceptance (T-036 REJECT). Added the literal failing string to the pass condition rather than only the positive clause, so the next reader can't satisfy the row by re-reading it charitably. Added a dated footnote under the table recording the provenance and stating plainly that fixing the shipped copy is `ios-developer`'s, not this amendment's.
  2. **`prds/live-events-overlay/TRD.md` §3.4** (T-034). New **clause 9** on the fixture authoring rule: within any one hour bucket, no two events share a coordinate, and no event name is templated (no stem-plus-index — `Fixture event 1…14`, `Event A`). Cites the concrete failure the PRD records: the shipped seed's `+5` bucket held 14 identically-named events at one identical point, satisfied clauses 1–8, and still rendered as 12 stacked identical markers. Added a provenance line naming `product`'s acceptance and the PRD's own D10 decisions-log row, and stating the shipped-fixture fix is T-051's, not here.
- **One thing I added beyond the brief, because the amendment is otherwise trap-shaped:** clause 9 explicitly names **clause 3's `markerCap + 2` bucket as where this bites.** Clause 3 requires one bucket to hold ≥14 events with distinct ranks to exercise truncation — that bucket *is* the `+5` bucket that failed, and it is also the one a demo is most likely to be opened on. Without the cross-reference, whoever satisfies clause 9 next could read "distinct ranks" as the whole obligation again and re-author the same degenerate bucket with fresh names. Clauses 3 and 9 are jointly satisfiable (14 distinct coordinates, 14 real names) — this is a note, not a new constraint.
- **Evidence:** documentation-only, so no build or test. Verified against source rather than the brief's description: read PRD req 6 bullet 1 and the events PRD's Dependencies paragraph + D10 decisions row directly to match wording, and confirmed `product`'s acceptance entry names exactly these two amendments and calls neither a re-review trigger.
- **Correction to the dispatch brief — no HTML twin exists for either TRD, and I did not create one.** The brief asked me to "regenerate the corresponding `.html` twin for each doc." There is nothing to regenerate: only 6 of 11 TRDs have HTML twins (`hood-dataset`, `hood-place-detail`, `live-events-pipeline`, `map-hoods-heat`, `places-dataset`, `time-slider`) and **neither `places-been-saved/TRD.md` nor `live-events-overlay/TRD.md` is among them** — verified with `ls */TRD.html`. There is also **no generator script anywhere in the repo** (the only `.sh` files are the `freeze`/`careful` skill hooks); twins are hand-authored. Minting two new HTML files would be authoring new artifacts, not regenerating, and would create a permanent sync obligation for two docs that never had one — precisely the stale-twin trap L-026/L-027 records, where `time-slider/TRD.html` sat stale and was then regenerated twice, independently and byte-identically, by two sessions ten minutes apart with neither result reaching `main`. Reporting this as not-done with the reason (rule 9), not silently skipped. **If HTML twins for these two are actually wanted, that's a real decision with an owner, not a step inside this amendment.**
- **Left behind:** (1) The T-036 empty-state **copy fix is still open and still `ios-developer`'s** — this amendment restores the bar `qa` tests against, it does not change a single shipped string. (2) The T-034 **fixture fix is still open and still T-051/`PAS-39`'s**; clause 9 now makes it falsifiable. (3) Neither TRD's `Status:` header line was bumped — both remain as they were, since a documentation reconciliation with no re-review owed is not a version event (T-049's amendment bumped to v2 because it struck a contract; this one strikes nothing). Flagging it in case a reader expects the T-049 pattern.

### 2026-08-03 — data-engineer — T-034/PAS-25: §4.2 query amendment confirmed correct as written

- **Did:** closed the last outstanding item from `product`'s 2026-08-03 acceptance pass on T-034 (the "D1 CONCUR" paragraph names `data-engineer` as still owing this). Read this file's T-034/T-035/T-036 acceptance entry (the "D1 CONCUR" paragraph) and `prds/live-events-overlay/TRD.md` §4.2 directly, per the dispatch brief — did not act on the summary alone. The ask: confirm (or fix) whether §4.2's query, as amended (dropping `start_at=gte.{anchorHour}`), actually implements the corrected **overlap** semantics D1 settled — "an event is visible if it overlaps the selected hour," not "starts within it" — rather than assume the amendment's own stated rationale is sufficient proof. This is a Build Phase 1 design-correctness check; no live query runs yet (T-043's pipeline is unbuilt), so verification is against the TRD's stated query shape only.
- **Verified by derivation, not by re-reading the amendment's own argument as its own proof.** §4.3's client-side predicate renders event at bucket `k` iff `startAt < bucketEnd(k) && endAt > bucketStart(k) && endAt > now`. Taking the union of that predicate over all 13 buckets (`k = 0…12`) that the client ever evaluates, the necessary-and-sufficient *superset* condition the fetch must satisfy collapses to exactly two bounds: `startAt < anchorHour + 13h` (from `k=12`'s `bucketEnd`) and `endAt > anchorHour` (from `k=0`'s `bucketStart`). Checked the amended query against that derived pair term for term: the query's own `start_at=lt.{anchorHour+13h}` matches the upper bound exactly, and `events_public`'s standing `end_at > now()` filter matches the lower bound given `anchorHour ≈ now()` at fetch time (session load or hour-roll refresh per §4.6/D11 — never more than seconds apart, immaterial at hour granularity). **Confirmed: the query as amended is correct.** The dropped `start_at=gte.{anchorHour}` clause was strictly tighter than the derived lower bound — it constrained `startAt` where the correct bound constrains `endAt` — so it necessarily excluded in-progress-at-open-time events (an event that started before `anchorHour` and is still running), which is exactly the failure mode D1's own rationale names. This confirms the drop was *necessary* for overlap semantics to be reachable at all, not merely a harmless simplification. No under-fetch found: every event that could satisfy the overlap predicate at any `k` survives the amended fetch. Rows the query returns that don't end up rendering at the currently-selected `k` are trimmed by §4.3's predicate client-side, which is correctness-neutral (over-fetch, not under-fetch). No SQL, view, index, or RLS policy implication follows from this — the view's existing filters were already doing the real work; the amendment only removes a redundant, over-tight client-side constraint.
- **No fix needed.** Nothing in T-043's `events_public` view or `live-events-pipeline/TRD.md` §4.2 changes as a result of this confirmation.
- **TRD edit:** added a confirmation note to `prds/live-events-overlay/TRD.md` §4.2 (immediately below the amendment paragraph, carrying the derivation above) and updated §11's `data-engineer` sign-off bullet from "to confirm at review" to "CONFIRMED correct as written, 2026-08-03." No requirement, contract, schema, or build step changed — this is the same class of documentation-only reconciliation as T-042's v2 amendment (no re-review owed).
- **Board:** updated both `BOARD.md` T-034 rows (Tasks table and V1 Build Phases table) to record the confirmation and point at the TRD note; T-034's acceptance status (`aviran-review`, PARTIAL reqs 1-5, req 6 → T-050) is otherwise unaffected — this closes only the one outstanding confirmation, not the task.
- **Left behind:** nothing new. T-050 (req 6 toggle + 400ms re-verification), T-051 (fixture bucket-5 plausibility fix), and T-052 (design-spec/raw-slug gaps) remain open and unaffected by this pass, per the acceptance entry above.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `prds/live-events-overlay/TRD.md`, `agent-os/BOARD.md` (T-034 rows, both tables), `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry). Explicit paths. Committed, not pushed — Aviran-gated, `CLAUDE.md` rule 9; commit hash reported in the final response.

**T-034 / `PAS-25` — ACCEPT, PARTIAL: reqs 1–5 only.**
- **Verified from source:** `EventSelection.selected` implements the overlap predicate, the `(rank desc, startAt asc, id asc)` total order and `prefix(12)` exactly as §4.3/§4.4 specify; `EventLayer` is a rounded rect + `sparkles` against `PlaceLayer`'s `Circle`, so req 1 bullet 4 survives greyscale by silhouette; `MapScreen.handleTap` runs `event → place → hood` with the event branch gated on `eventStore.isLayerVisible`, the same gate the `ForEach` draw uses — rendering and hit-testing share one gate, which is the rule T-033's own acceptance REJECT produced; `EventDetailRows.rows(for:)` makes req 4's omit-don't-blank structural (a nil field cannot produce a row); the empty/unreachable path degrades to `events == []` with no banner.
- **Four calls ruled. D1 CONCUR** — req 1 bullet 1 stated a *sufficient* condition and an implementer could legitimately have read it as exclusive; the literal reading makes a four-hour event visible for one of its four hours, answering a different question than strategy's "what's happening" at the selected hour. PRD bullet rewritten to the overlap wording. `data-engineer` still owes the §4.2 confirmation (dropping `start_at=gte`) — unchanged by this ruling. **D4 RULED, architect's option (a)** — req 2 bullet 2 moves to `T-041`/`PAS-30` and T-041's board row now names it. Not waived: the screen-distance rule is unimplemented app-wide, owned by no PRD, and at ≤12 capped markers there is nothing to cluster; blocking a Phase-1 task on an unowned Phase-2 one bought nothing. **D2 CONFIRMED**, and on stronger grounds than the TRD gave: I read `map-rendering-spec.md` §1–§2 directly, and the "Pins: none at city-wide" row is about dozens of place pins per Hood, while §1's own argument for moving the flag to a city-wide-visible channel is *"heat alone at cold open is the half of this product anyone already gets from Google Maps"* — an events layer invisible at cold-open zoom repeats exactly that failure. The cap and the zoom rule are one decision; overturning the cap reopens this. **D10 CONFIRMED** with an added plausibility clause (below).
- **Two things I did NOT accept, carried to `aviran-review` explicitly rather than folded into a pass.** (1) **Req 6 (layer toggle) is entirely unbuilt.** C12 is blocked on T-032's `HeatModalCard`; `grep -rn "HeatModalCard"` returns only comments referencing it, no type. `isLayerVisible` exists and both the draw and the hit-test honour it, but nothing user-reachable sets it — a P0 with no UI. (2) **Req 1 bullet 2's 400ms hour-change budget is unverified, and `qa` marked it PASS.** TEST-PLAN row 1b cites `ColdOpenPerformanceTests`, which I read: it measures the `ColdOpenToInteractive` signpost (process start → first pannable frame), a different milestone. No `HourRepaint` signpost exists in the committed tree (`HeatRepaintSignpost.swift` is T-032's, still untracked) and no hour control ships. Corrected to BLOCKED, same class as rows 6a–6c. **Neither is a build defect** — nobody can fix either until T-032 lands, so a REJECT to `build` would be theatre. Both are carried on **T-050** with an owner, and T-034 is not complete until it closes.
- **Fixture, verified by parsing it rather than reading the review's claim:** all 8 authoring clauses hold (6 distinct buckets, bucket 4 empty, bucket 5 = cap+2 at distinct ranks, `seed-0001` spanning 3 buckets, null `venue_name`/`category` present, `seed-0018` already ended, fictional names). `qa`'s bucket-5 finding is real and worse than "cosmetic": 14 rows named "Fixture event 1…14" at one identical coordinate render as 12 stacked identical markers at that hour. Added a plausibility requirement to the PRD (within one bucket, no shared coordinate, no templated name) and filed **T-051**.

**T-035 / `PAS-26` — ACCEPT.**
- **Verified from source:** `TouristFlag`'s three storage → two rendered states; `HoodLayer.effectiveStroke` forcing `.none` at `.cityWide` regardless of flag, matching §2's table row for row; `.plain` 2.5pt solid vs `.busyWarning` 3pt dashed — distinct without hue, and 2.5pt vs the unflagged 0.5pt hairline likewise; `FlagCopy`/`HoodSpeech` carry no "tourist trap" string and none of decision #18's retired vocabulary; the place-modal line renders iff `isTouristTrap == true`; `PlaceLayer`'s label announces provenance but never the flag; `LocalQAGate.decide` implements the answered → notifications-denied → rolling-24h precedence with the order itself as the contract; req 9 is structural — neither `FlagStroke.treatment(for:band:)` nor `HoodSpeech.label(name:band:flag:)` can take answer-store data, so an answer cannot reach a rendered flag. A1's data landed: parsed `hoods-tel-aviv.json` — 2 flagged (`florentin`, `kerem-hateimanim`), 1 explicit `false` (`ramat-aviv`), 21 `null`; all three storage states demonstrable.
- **D3 CONFIRMED — the requirement was wrong, not the build.** Req 2 bullet 3 ("moving the slider… changes no stroke") and req 5 (busy+flagged is per-hour by definition) cannot both be true. `map-rendering-spec.md` §3 locks the busy+flagged treatment as a *replacement* of the plain flagged stroke, which is only coherent under the TRD's reading. **Rewrote req 2 bullet 3** to what actually holds: the flagged *set* is time-invariant and no unflagged Hood ever gains a stroke; an already-flagged Hood's stroke *style* varies with the hour.
- **D5 CONFIRMED, with the missing pass condition written in (L-009).** Req 4 bullet 2's "resolved on tap by the Hood sheet" named no surface and no strings, and `HoodSheet.swift` had no flag element at all — a requirement no gate could fail. The TRD's three-state line is the only construction that distinguishes not-flagged from not-yet-rated, which req 4 bullet 3 and req 7 both depend on. Req 4 bullet 2 now carries the three strings as its pass condition.
- **Req 5 confirmed as honestly named, not silently passed.** Verified independently rather than taking the TRD's word: `Passenger/Resources/` contains no density seed, and `BuildPhase` has no density constant, so `band` is `nil` for every Hood at every hour and `.busyWarning` cannot render. Built and unit-covered at the pure-function layer; re-verify when T-032's C10 lands.
- **§1's Phase-1 boundary CONFIRMED** (this was also outstanding for `product`, beyond the two calls in the brief): req 8's notification + geofence half is legitimately out of Build Phase 1 — no dwell detector and no `UserNotifications` link exists anywhere by design. It now has a board row and an owner it did not have when this PRD was written (`T-046`/`PAS-33`); bullets 1 and 6 and the "syncs later" half of bullet 5 re-verify there and at B1. Everything else in req 8 is real and verified.
- **Left open, not waived:** §9 row 8(f)'s live VoiceOver focus run (implementation confirmed correct by grep — never `makeKeyAndVisible`, one `.announcement`, zero `.screenChanged` — but no runtime assertion). Same environment-access class as T-034's X3.

**T-036 / `PAS-27` — REJECT → `build`. One blocking finding.**
- **F1 (blocking): PRD req 6 bullet 1 fails.** `PlacesListEmptyState.swift` renders an icon, "Nothing here yet", and an "Explore the map" CTA. The requirement is *"a plain empty state **naming what would fill it**"* — the shipped copy names nothing. Fix is one string: state the ways a place gets here (save one from its detail modal, or spend time at one). **Why blocking rather than polish:** V1 forbids onboarding, so this empty state is the only surface where the feature can explain itself, and it is exactly what a first-run user sees when they tap Places. **Why no earlier gate caught it:** TRD §9 row 6's pass condition is "icon, line, and a CTA that dismisses the list — not an error string, not a spinner" — it dropped the PRD's "naming what would fill it" clause, so `qa` tested the weaker row and had nothing to fail. That is a spec defect as much as a build defect (L-009): I sharpened the PRD bullet in the same commit as this verdict, and `architect` owes §9 row 6 the matching clause.
- **F2 (fold into the same pass, non-blocking):** remove `PlacesListRow.touristHeavySlot` — a reserved `@ViewBuilder` returning `EmptyView()`, waiting for a filler that is now ruled out (below). Dead code with a comment promising a future that will not arrive.
- **Everything else PASSES, re-derived from source:** `PlacesListComposition.entries` makes precedence a structural `max` over `PlaceProvenance: Comparable` with `provenance` non-optional per entry, so "exactly one word, never two, never none" is a property of the type; one row per place by construction; `SavedPlacesStore.toggle` mutates in memory synchronously with a generation-guarded fire-and-forget write, so the 400ms budget is met by construction and a reordered write cannot land stale; the closed badge reads `place.permanentlyClosed`, never substitutes for anything, never removes the row or disables the route; the ring is dashed (shape, not colour alone), close-zoom only, drawn *outside* the 44pt frame; three dismissal paths on the list; row tap → `openPlace` with `chrome.presented` structurally untouched.
- **Four calls ruled, all CONFIRMED, none affected by the REJECT. D4** — name-ascending: the PRD specifies no order at all, so this fills a gap rather than deviating; recency would need a timestamp the current record shape does not carry, i.e. a versioned-payload migration on already-shipped accepted state. If Aviran wants recency it is a schema change, not a sort change. **D5** — no offline banner: not a silent gap; a banner would announce a sync V1 never performs. The PRD's own Technical design already said "Writes nothing server-side in V1," so **req 6 bullet 2's "and syncs later" was the defect** — struck, with the condition for restoring it stated. **D7** — checked `ux-flows.md` line 46 directly rather than trusting the citation: *"Saved-places icon (4th icon) — persistent icon, **separate from the 3 nav buttons**."* D7 is right and T-032's D1 was wrong. The accepted cost (the icon fades and stops hit-testing under any presented surface, so it cannot dismiss its own list) is acceptable — req 8 bullet 2 asks only for mutual exclusivity and the list carries three dismissal paths. The related wrinkle D7 does not name — Places is unreachable *from* an open heat/search/Passport surface, unlike the nav row's direct switching — goes to the post-ship designer pass (T-052). **§9 row 5's framing** — honest, confirmed: grepped `Places/` and `PlacesList/` for `LocationStore` (zero) and `CoreLocation` (only `CLLocationCoordinate2D` types). The absence of a location code path is genuinely what req 5 protects — no location-derived row can appear when permission is denied because no row is location-derived at all. It is *not* the runtime behaviour the bullet describes (the fixture populates regardless of permission), and that is Build Phase 1's declared fake-data scope, re-verified at `T-046`. Recorded in the PRD's decisions log so it can't be read later as a pass.
- **Cross-PRD contradiction found and ruled:** `tourist-trap-flag` req 6 says the place flag is one line in the detail modal *"and nowhere else"* (`map-rendering-spec.md` §4 agrees: *"The flag lives in the spot sheet as a line of text, full stop"*), while `places-been-saved` req 4 bullet 3 and its row-density open question both assume a tourist-heavy line on a Places row. The build satisfied both by shipping a permanently-empty slot. **Ruled: T-035 req 6 governs** — the row never carries the line; req 4 bullet 3's independence check lives in the detail modal. Both PRDs updated; the row-density risk is resolved as a consequence (worst case is a provenance word plus one badge, within `design-principles.md` §2).
- **Evidence, all three:** source read at `passenger-code` HEAD `a1a0c71`-era tree (`3d8e3b8` + the T-037 C7 fix), with `952cf2f`/`6139f13`/`24eb533` confirmed as ancestors. Fixtures parsed with `python3`, not eyeballed. No `xcodebuild` run this pass — `qa` ran the full suite green on these commits twice this day (343/343 and 253/253 + 6/6) and I re-verified the specific claims that mattered against source instead of re-running it.
- **PRD status lines flipped in the same commit as the verdicts (L-006):** T-034 → Accepted (Build Phase 1, reqs 1–5, req 6 explicitly not accepted); T-035 → Accepted (Build Phase 1); T-036 stays Draft v1, rejected.
- **Left behind:** (1) **T-050** — T-034's req 6 build (C12, fully specified in its TRD §4.8/§11, no new TRD work) plus the 400ms re-verification; blocked on T-032's C5, owner `ios-developer`. **T-034 is not complete until it closes — say so at `aviran-review`.** (2) **T-051** — two fixture gaps (events bucket 5; no place that is both closed and flagged). (3) **T-052** — `map-rendering-spec.md` carries no event-marker rules, and `EventDetailModal` renders raw `hoodID`/category slugs; `designer`'s. (4) **`architect` owes two TRD amendments, neither urgent, neither a re-review:** T-036 §9 row 6 must carry the PRD's "naming what would fill it" clause, and T-034 §3.4's authoring rule needs the plausibility clause added as clause 9. (5) All three need Linear issues for T-050/T-051/T-052 — `chief-of-staff`'s, no Linear write made here. (6) `data-engineer`'s confirmation of T-034 TRD §4.2's query amendment is still outstanding and unaffected by this pass.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `prds/live-events-overlay/live-events-overlay.md`, `prds/tourist-trap-flag/tourist-trap-flag.md`, `prds/places-been-saved/places-been-saved.md`, `agent-os/BOARD.md`, `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry). Explicit paths, no `-A`. Split into **two commits** deliberately. `PROGRESS.md` re-read at `--staged`: one hunk, 49 inserted lines, all mine. **`BOARD.md` is NOT clean and is committed separately with full disclosure (rule 2):** it carries two hunks that are **`ios-code-reviewer`'s, not mine** — its T-038/`PAS-29` code-review verdict (APPROVE WITH MINORS on `passenger-code 3d8e3b8`), written to both the compact V1-Build-Phases row and the lifecycle row while this pass was running, with its own worklog entry not yet landed. The tree held only my three PRD edits when I started, so those hunks appeared mid-pass. Rule 2's letter says unstage a shared file whose hunks aren't all mine; here that would strand three acceptance verdicts and three new task rows (T-050/T-051/T-052) that the board depends on, and the last time a `product` board row was left unstaged mid-pass it was swept into another session's commit anyway and disclosed only afterwards (this file, same date). - **Did:** QA pass on `passenger-code 952cf2f` — the combined fix landing `ios-code-reviewer`'s two APPROVE-WITH-MINORS findings from T-035's own review (`MapScreen.swift`'s `.task` race, `PlaceDetailModal.swift`'s `Label`-vs-`HStack` a11y gap), mixed in the same commit with unrelated T-036/PAS-36/PAS-27 fixes per the dispatch (ignored those, per brief). Read `BOARD.md`'s T-035 row and `PROGRESS.md`'s recent worklog (the T-034 QA entry, which flagged `DebugVisitSourceTests` — T-035's own test — crashing in a full-suite run and spawned `task_aed5ee1f` for T-035's owner, unconfirmed) before starting. **`prds/tourist-trap-flag/TEST-PLAN.md` did not exist** — wrote it this pass from `TRD.md` §9's verification table (one row per P0 sub-check, 20 rows total incl. both minor-fix verifications and the full-suite/flake rows). It should have existed since `trd`/`build`, per this agent's own "prepare early" rule; written retroactively against already-shipped code rather than ahead of it.
- **Both code-review minors confirmed fixed, by direct read, not by trusting the commit message:**
  1. `MapScreen.swift:296-303` — `localQACoordinator.loadPersistedState()` then `.start()` now sequenced in one `.task` block (`await loadPersistedState(); await start()`), replacing the pre-fix two independent `.task` modifiers. Read `LocalQACoordinator.swift` to confirm the race was real: `start()`'s `handle(_:)` reads `answerStore.answeredPlaceIDs`/`lastAskedAt` on every incoming `VisitEvent`, both populated only by `loadPersistedState()`'s disk read — under the old two-`.task` ordering, SwiftUI gives no guarantee `start()` won't begin consuming events before the sibling `.task`'s `await` resolves. **Not independently unit- or UI-testable as a race**: `MapScreen` is a View, both calls are fast in-process async calls with no injectable delay to force the old ordering to actually manifest a bug either way — noted in TEST-PLAN row X1 rather than claimed covered by a test that doesn't exist. Got circumstantial live confirmation instead: repeatedly launched the app (`xcrun simctl launch … -simulateLocalQAVisit florentin-anna-loulou-bar`) and observed the rolling-24h daily cap correctly suppress the toast across relaunches within the window, then correctly re-offer it after `-uiTestResetLocalQA` wiped the ledger — screenshotted the toast rendering ("Does this feel like a tourist-heavy spot?" / No / Yes, top-anchored, non-blocking, map still interactive underneath, exactly matching TRD D4/req 8). That suppression only holds if the persisted `lastAskedAt` load completes before the gate check runs on the ~1s-delayed debug event — which is what the fix guarantees and the old ordering didn't.
  2. `PlaceDetailModal.swift:80-87` — `touristTrapSlot` now reads `Label(FlagCopy.placeLine, systemImage: "camera.fill")`. Read the full file: this is byte-pattern-identical to the sibling `categoryRow` (line 71-74)'s own `Label(place.category.displayName, systemImage:)` — one VoiceOver stop, matching every sibling row in the file, confirmed by construction rather than a live VoiceOver run (a `Label` collapses icon+text into one accessibility element by SwiftUI's own semantics; no test needed beyond confirming the type used).
- **Full `xcodebuild build` + `xcodebuild test`** (scheme Passenger, iPhone 17 Pro simulator `60E0B96F…`, not scoped — `PassengerTests` + `PassengerUITests` together, per the standing full-target rule): **BUILD SUCCEEDED**, zero errors. `PassengerTests`: **343/343 passed**, zero failures — including both `DebugVisitSourceTests` cases and `LocalQAInstallIdentityTests`. The crash T-034's 2026-08-03 QA entry flagged against `DebugVisitSourceTests.argumentEmitsOneDebugEvent()` (spawned as background `task_aed5ee1f` for this task's owner, never confirmed real) **did not reproduce this run** — resolves as a flake on this evidence; `task_aed5ee1f` looks closeable, though closing it isn't this session's to do.
- **`PassengerUITests`: 3 failures in the full run**, all three carrying the identical "Restarting after unexpected exit, crash, or test timeout" signature at the exact point of failure, none an assertion failure: `ColdOpenPerformanceTests.testColdOpenToInteractiveSignpost()` (the already-documented flake, cited across ≥4 prior `PROGRESS.md` entries — not re-derived, per L-010), `EventDetailSheetInteractionTests.testTappingEventMarkerOpensEventDetailSheetNotAHoodSheet()` (T-034's, not this task's to own), and **`LocalQAToastInteractionTests.testIgnoringTheToastAutoDismissesWithNoReminderThisVisit()` — T-035's own C14 UI test.** Triaged the last one directly rather than leaving it as a worklog line (L-010): re-ran it twice more in isolation — the full `LocalQAToastInteractionTests` class crashed again with the identical signature (plus a simulator-emitted warning about a duplicate `UIAccessibilityLoaderWebShared` class across `WebCore`/`WebKit` accessibility bundles, a known simulator-runtime issue, not app code), then the single method alone, run by itself, **passed clean in 13.4s**. 2 of 3 runs clean, and the one failure's signature is identical to the long-documented `ColdOpenPerformanceTests` class — this is the same environmental crash-under-resource-pressure pattern, now confirmed to also hit UI test suites beyond the one it was previously pinned to, not a regression from either fix in this commit. Flagged via `spawn_task` for separate tracking (the pattern spreading to more suites in one run is new information worth a look) rather than re-deriving root cause a fifth time.
- **TRD §9's other Build-Phase-1-checkable P0 rows** (full table in `TEST-PLAN.md`): read `HoodLayer.swift` in full to confirm req 2's fill/stroke channel separation and req 3's zoom-tier disclosure (`effectiveStroke` hard-forces `.none` at `.cityWide` regardless of the pure `treatment()` result, matching D1's correction) — both PASS. ### 2026-08-03 — product — T-049/PAS-37 ruling: bundled-vs-fetched `place_type` registry — **amend T-042 to match T-037 D4 (option 2)**

- **Did:** documentation-reconciliation ruling dispatched by `chief-of-staff` (round 4, relay). Read `BOARD.md` in full (T-037/T-042/T-049 rows), this file's recent worklog (no "Current Snapshot" section exists — the documented exception at `PROGRESS.md:830`), then **both disputed sections in full and their surrounding documents**: `prds/passport/TRD.md` §1 open-items table, §2.1, §3.3, §3.4/D4, §11 A1, §12; `prds/places-dataset/TRD.md` §2.2, §3.1, §3.3, §4.2, §4.4, §4.5, §5, §9, §10. Did **not** rule from the ticket description's summary of either.
- **Verdict — option 2. The registry is bundled; `GET /rest/v1/place_types` comes out of T-042's client fetch contract.** Three independent grounds, any one sufficient:
  1. **Scope ownership.** T-042 §2.2 assigns the client half of C-STICKER-1 to T-037 by name, and §9 states *"No `[iOS]` step exists in this task — every client-side consequence is another task's amendment."* §4.5's *"One extra small GET for `place_types` … fetched once per session alongside the places load"* is a client-runtime behaviour claim inside a document that says it specs no client behaviour. D4 governs by scope, not by which doc ships or gets referenced last.
  2. **T-042 already contradicts itself, and its own §4.2 sides with D4.** §4.2 defines the client half as *"a test asserting every `sticker_shape` present in the shipped `place-types.json` **export** resolves to a real asset."* A shipped export is a bundled build-time artifact — D4's design, written into T-042 three sections before §4.5 specs a runtime GET. Amending §4.5 makes T-042 self-consistent; amending D4 would leave T-042 contradicting itself and break §4.2's own falsifiable pass condition, which is only checkable against a bundled file.
  3. **D4's argument is unrebutted and nothing needs the GET.** A `sticker_shape` key is undrawable unless the binary ships the asset; an unknown key renders `.generic` however it arrived, so fetching buys an undrawable key plus a request, a cache field and a skew class. Both `data-engineer` and `code-reviewer` APPROVEd A1/A2 and flagged only the unreconciled-docs process gap, neither contested the reasoning. T-042 names no consumer of that endpoint: the pipeline runs authored source → validator → export → DB seed, so the DB is downstream of the mapping, never upstream of it.
- **Options 1 and 3 rejected explicitly, not by omission.** (1) "Intentionally different concerns" is not a coherent story here — one client, one registry, one mapping; "the server offers it and the client never calls it" is a dead endpoint documented as a contract, which is what gets built by whoever reads §4.5 without reading passport §3.4. (3) Phase-2-scoping is worse than doing nothing: D4 is explicitly *not* Build-Phase scoped (*"it holds permanently for V1"*), and deferring parks the contradiction at the precise moment nobody re-reads this ticket, handing the Phase-2 developer two approved docs that still disagree.
- **No code change, confirmed against source not assumed.** T-037's in-flight build is building D4 as written — A1 already landed (`passenger-code cc2b008`, `Passenger/Resources/place-types-tel-aviv.json`, six types, bundled). T-042 is HELD/undispatched. Nothing already shipped moves.
- **Second finding — new, not in `PAS-37`'s description: the bundled registry has no emitter, and until now had two names.** The shipped `place-types-tel-aviv.json` header reads *"Regenerate/replace, do not extend by hand"* — and **no generator exists that could regenerate it.** Verified directly: `database/scripts/` holds `build_hoods.py`, `export_hoods_geojson.py`, `validate_dataset.py`, `test_hood_containment_fixture.py` — no `export_places.py` (T-042 B3, unbuilt), no `places-tel-aviv.source.json`. T-042 §4.2 calls the artifact an "export," implying generated; T-042's build breakdown emits only `places-tel-aviv.json` + the DB seed. At Phase 2, when B1's vocabulary is ratified and `place_types` holds real rows, the bundle and the table can diverge with nothing detecting it, and whoever updates the bundle hand-edits a file the TRD calls an export — **L-024's exact failure mode, one phase away, with a hand-edit ban already printed in the file's own header and no way to obey it.** Reconciling §4.5 without closing this just moves the hole. Also the standing PRD data/schema rule doing its job: a bundled artifact with no named emitter is exactly the implicit data need that must be explicit before someone builds or sources it.
- **Amendment owed by `architect`** (product rules, architect writes the TRD — T-033's precedent: *"`product` made the call (`30e002f`), `architect` amended the TRD"*). T-042 → **v2**: (a) §4.5 remove the `place_types` GET and the "fetched once per session" sentence, replace with the bundled statement linking `passport/TRD.md` §3.4/D4 — **keep `place_type` inside the embedded `hoods?select=…places(…)` select**, that is per-row data and must stay live at Phase 2; (b) §3.3 keep `place_types_public_read` as written, add one line that the V1 client is not its reader (removing a policy churns a migration for no gain); (c) §4.2 fix the artifact name `place-types.json` → `place-types-tel-aviv.json`, matching what actually shipped and the `<thing>-tel-aviv.json` convention — verified against `main`, not chosen; (d) Decisions-log row citing T-049/`PAS-37` + passport D4, v2 bump; (e) same commit, flip the stale `**Status:** ready for trd-review` line (it cleared `trd-review` approved-as-is) — L-006's discipline applied to a TRD; (f) same commit, `prds/places-dataset/TRD.html` carries the old §4.2/§4.5 text and goes stale the instant the `.md` changes — regenerate it, or say so and open a `BOARD.md` row **with an owner**, never "flagged for whoever owns it" (that phrasing is what produced L-026/L-027); (g) name the emitter per the second finding — recommended shape, mechanism is architect's call: B3's `export_places.py` emits the registry from the same validated source that produces the `place_types` seed rows (one source, two artifacts, §5 step 3's existing rule), and T-037's A1 file is stated as a hand-authored Phase-1 placeholder the generator replaces at Phase 2, with L-024's ban attaching from that point forward.
- **Minor on the T-037 side — prose only, no contract change, no re-review, build untouched:** D4's line *"This is a deviation from T-042 §4.2/§4.5's implied client fetch and is flagged for T-042's reviewers"* is wrong on two counts now — §4.2 was never a client fetch (it already describes a shipped export), so the deviation is from §4.5 alone; and the flag is resolved, so it should name this ruling and date instead. `ios-developer`/`data-engineer` need not re-read.
- **Scope gate (Job 0), run on inherited work:** clean. T-049 authorizes no feature and adds no scope — it reconciles two documents belonging to features already carrying verbatim strategy citations (`passport.md` Motivation quotes the stamp-per-Been-place and Profile-tab lines; T-042 is the dataset those features read). Nothing here touches social, onboarding, or a parked phase.
- **Left behind:** (1) **T-049 must not close until the amendment lands** — a ruling in a Linear comment is not a reconciled document; the two TRDs still contradict each other on disk as of this entry. (2) `architect` not dispatched by me — `chief-of-staff` holds dispatch and is the only Linear writer; the amendment list above is the brief. (3) The emitter gap (second finding) is the item most likely to be dropped, because it reads as adjacent to the ticket's question rather than part of it — it is not: it is the same defect one phase later. (4) Untouched and still open, named so they aren't read as resolved by this ruling: T-042 B1's Aviran-gated vocabulary ratification (`PAS-6` item 11), and §4.4's standing **[ASSUMPTION]** that `place_type` is internal-only, which passport D12 also leans on.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry), explicit path. Tree was clean at start (`git status --short` empty at `55c7f1c`), so this commit carries no other session's hunks. No `passenger-code` change — ruling requires none. No Linear write (`chief-of-staff` is the single writer). Not pushed — Aviran-gated, rule 9. Hash in the final response.
- **Disclosure, rule 2, reverse direction:** my `agent-os/BOARD.md` T-049 rewrite was written to disk and then **swept into a concurrent `chief-of-staff` session's commit `passenger-brain 3e349c7`** ("round 4 addendum: ios-code-reviewer APPROVEs 952cf2f") before I could stage it — that commit's own edit was to the T-036/PAS-36 rows, and its message does not name the carried T-049 hunk. Verified, not assumed: `git show HEAD:agent-os/BOARD.md` contains my ruling text and `git status` reports `BOARD.md` unmodified, so **the row is on `main` and correct** — nothing is stranded and no re-edit is needed. Recording it here because rule 2's disclosure requirement is about the commit message, and the agent that could have written it didn't know it was carrying my text. This is the same class as the four 2026-08-02 commits, seen from the other side: the shared-tree window is wide enough that an edit-then-stage sequence loses the race, so stage in the same breath as the edit on `BOARD.md`, don't batch it with a later file.
