### 2026-08-03 — retrospective — nightly run covering 2026-08-02 from 19:20 on: 3 lessons (L-026, L-027, L-023 re-fixed), all applied; 1 fix confirmed working within hours

- **Window.** Per L-020's step-0 rule, read `LESSONS.md`'s newest header first: `## 2026-08-02`, written by a run that closed at 19:21. The day did not stop there — **45 more `passenger-brain` commits and 3 `passenger-code` commits landed between 19:00 and 20:51**, so the window opened at 19:20 rather than at midnight. That rule is the only reason this run isn't a short-circuit. The tree went live again mid-run (`75d8926` project-manager 00:07, `d6ab04b` analytics-engineer 00:10); both recorded as found, neither chased.
- **The night's theme, and why it stopped being deferrable.** Last night's report named the concurrency rate and deferred it as "infrastructural, not a rule gap." Within four hours it cost **duplicated work and lost work on the same incident**: two sessions in two isolated worktrees regenerated the *byte-identical* time-slider HTML twins ten minutes apart (`9c1006c` 20:27, `5dfd812` 20:37 — `git diff` between them is empty), and **neither reached `main`**, along with a third stranded commit (`03dab7d`). All four stale HTML twins are still stale on `main` tonight. Separately, two `data-engineer` sessions independently completed T-035/PAS-26's A1 `trd-review`, both APPROVE WITH MINORS — "that's luck, not process," as the second one wrote when it found the first.
- **L-026 — an isolated worktree delivers nothing** (process, general, applied). "I committed it" reads the same whether the commit is on `main` or on a branch nobody will check out, so the author gets no signal. → `passenger-brain/CLAUDE.md` **new rule 10**: a commit is delivered when it is on `main`; merge the `claude/*` branch before the turn ends, or declare it stranded in the report *and* a `BOARD.md` row. → `project-manager.md` **check F** gains a stranded-branch sweep in both repos, with two branches touching the same files as a separate hit. Detection is a dispatch-chief-of-staff item, not a fix-silently one — merging someone else's branch is a content call.
- **L-027 — a claim at task granularity doesn't lock work dispatched at step granularity** (process, general, applied). PAS-26 splits `trd-review` across three pairs; its `owner:*` labels and BOARD.md owner field describe the *task*, so both sessions read a correctly-claimed row and both dispatched A1. L-007's "re-read fresh before dispatching" was followed and could not help — the row never recorded which step was taken. → `chief-of-staff.md`: a split task's BOARD.md row carries **one claim line per step**, and those lines, not the owner field, are what gets read before dispatching a step. The second half is the same failure at zero — the twin regeneration was never a row at all, flagged twice as "for whoever owns it" — → `CLAUDE.md` **rule 5** extended: a defect you find but don't own becomes a row with a name on it, not a remark.
- **L-023 — re-fixed, previously `applied` → `failed`.** Rule 2's "unstage the file and say whose it is" landed at 19:23; in the next ninety minutes **four commits each carried other sessions' worklog entries** (`0228391`, `d3fa249` with six, `4a96756`, `96b293b`), and `2c823ba` is a commit whose entire content is a line disclosing one of them. The detection half worked — agents noticed every time. The remedy half **cannot be obeyed**: unstaging `PROGRESS.md` means your own entry never lands. Every agent independently invented the same substitute, which makes the written rule the thing that's wrong. → `CLAUDE.md` **rule 2** carve-out: for an append-only shared log, commit it and name every carried entry and its author agent **in the original message**; a follow-up disclosure commit is the failure that replaces. Watch item: a fifth undisclosed sweep means the single-file worklog itself is the problem, and the next fix is structural — too large to auto-apply.
- **Verified:** **L-018** (TRD §9 Verification, applied last night) is the fastest adoption in `LESSONS.md` — present in all four TRDs touched tonight, including a retrofit into T-032's, and already used as a gate (`ios-code-reviewer` blocked T-035 partly on a §10 citation with no matching §9 row). **L-021** clean: zero hits for stale `origin brain`/`HEAD:brain`/`locali` references across the live agent tree and both mirrors. **L-017** survived the design-gate pivot editing agent files after last night's sweep — three-way `diff -qr` clean apart from the known `agents-mirror/chief.md` orphan. **L-022/L-024/L-025/L-007/L-011/L-002** all held; details in `LESSONS.md`'s step-4 section.
- **Left behind:** (1) **Three stranded commits on two `claude/*` branches** in `passenger-brain`, and four stale HTML twins on `main` — not merged by this run (hygiene is `project-manager`/`chief-of-staff`'s, and reviewing three sessions' regenerated documents is a content call). Tomorrow's check F will catch it; naming it here so it doesn't wait. (2) **`passenger-code` has uncommitted T-032 build work** (`Passenger/Map/SettingsHint.swift` modified, `Passenger/Support/HeatRepaintSignpost.swift` untracked) — a live session's, left untouched. (3) **No PM digest was available** when evidence was gathered; `nightly-pm-audit` fired 23:18 and was still in flight, landing `75d8926` at 00:07. Not a failure — noted because L-020's flagged-for-Aviran liveness alert would have answered "running or dead?" in a second instead of by process inspection. (4) `agents-mirror/chief.md`, the mis-filed `/chief` slash command, still has no live counterpart — `project-manager`'s check G, unchanged from last night.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `CLAUDE.md`, `agent-os/LESSONS.md`, `agent-os/agents-mirror/chief-of-staff.md`, `agent-os/agents-mirror/project-manager.md`, `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry), all by explicit path. **`agent-os/BOARD.md` was already staged in the index by a concurrent session (8 insertions / 8 deletions) and was not touched, staged, or committed** — rule 2. `passenger-code` — `.claude/agents/chief-of-staff.md`, `.claude/agents/project-manager.md` (the mirror copies only); the two in-flight Swift files above deliberately left alone. Committed, not pushed — pushing is Aviran-gated, rule 9.
